# PROGRESS LEDGER — read this first in any new session / account

**Purpose.** The owner switches Claude accounts at unpredictable times. This file + `docs/PLAN.md` are the hand-off:
everything needed to continue lives in this folder, not in a chat. Refresh with `python3 tools/progress.py`
(`--log "…"` appends to the hand log). A lane counts as LANDED only when its output file exists on disk.

**STOP POINT (owner, 2026-09-21): after the Phase-3 critic lands, a handoff is written (`docs/HANDOFF.md`) and work PAUSES.
The owner will start the adversarial review (workflow 03) at a later date — do NOT launch it without their go-ahead.**

**How to resume.**
1. Run `python3 tools/progress.py` and read the tables below: any lane "not landed" must be (re)run.
2. Workflow run caches do NOT travel between sessions/accounts. Re-run only missing lanes: the workflow scripts are
   saved in `workflows/` (each reader resolves its own slice from `research/worklists.json`); delete finished lanes from
   the `TASKS` array or spawn the missing ones as individual agents with the same prompts.
3. Standing owner directives (2026-09-21, staffing widened 2026-09-25): Fable = manager, judgment (critics/judges/assessors), architect, validator (red team); Opus = verifier, recon/reader, implementer, surveyor — managed by Fable · architecture is COMPLETELY
   STANDALONE (nothing from `settlement-engine` as input or constraint) · improve on the references, not just recreate ·
   record progress here regularly · approvals do not travel between accounts — re-confirm anything outward-facing ·
   PRODUCT SCOPE (2026-09-25): base = MFCG/FTG fusion + own improvements, built first; then the city composed of NG-class
   neighbourhoods; the village is a separate later product · at most four lanes running at a time ·
   ROADMAP (2026-09-25): standalone generators first (town/city base → neighbourhoods → village) → reconcile with the
   historical + artistic corpus in ~/Desktop/settlement-engine/map-corpus (the built generator stays the BASE) → only
   then SettlementForge inputs; the no-settlement-engine veto stays in force until the owner opens the reconcile step ·
   DESIGN TIEBREAKER (2026-09-25): MFCG-vs-FTG conflicts resolve toward realism — explicit constraints, precise
   real-world geometry — with legibility as the floor · STAGE 3 (2026-09-25): buildings inside neighbourhoods built
   Fantasy-Manor-style (compositional footprints visible at every zoom) with diversity manor→hovel decided by
   explicit per-plot rules held as data tables; base keeps the hooks (per-lot context vector, interface, LOD) only ·
   PLOT MODEL + GRANULARITY (2026-09-25, BASE): plots hold 0..n buildings + yard; site-plan rules (row-house frontages
   with party walls; multi-building institution plots; hovel in a yard); wealth/assignment join plots (prosperous) or
   split them (poor); alleys/back lanes emerge between plots; footprints + yards drawn, never plots as buildings ·
   EVERYTHING EDITABLE (2026-09-25): every element and every rule table/preset editable by users and by us; edits
   persist, undo, survive regeneration, migrate; local repair ·
   PROSPERITY GRADIENT (2026-09-25): wealth = continuous field (predictable drivers + controlled chance), banded only
   where rules need classes; continuous rules interpolate; dynamic over time; editable ·
   SIZE LADDERS (2026-09-25): town/city = town → city → metropolis; village = thorp → hamlet → village ·
   ASSIGNMENT HOOK (2026-09-25, for the host-input phase): names/functions/shapes assignable with drift bounds to one
   building, a whole neighbourhood, or N dispersed buildings — stable ids + assignment input + placement solver ·
   CITY ADD-ON LAYERS (2026-09-25, toggles, after the city is mastered): SEWERS derived by medieval-appropriate rules;
   FLOATING ISLANDS (1–3 organic oval sky landmasses of neighbourhoods); base keeps layer/drainage/outline hooks ·
   EVOLUTION HOOK (2026-09-25, much later): settlements grow/evolve over decades (wall → ring road, new outer curtain);
   base keeps lifecycles/versions, an incremental pipeline mode, first-class conversions, per-epoch RNG sub-streams;
   coherent and from the SAME SEED (clarified: the seed = the original town; the INPUTS determine the history as
   modifications/variations of it; epochs derive from the previous state; no re-rolls).
4. Never re-scrape or re-record what the "Archives"/"Captures" lists already show. FTG allows one new settlement per
   IP per day (ours: "Galeiaward", 2026-09-21 ~05:17 UTC, re-served for 24 h).

RULE (the owner's): (1) drawing a thing or dragging it holds nothing — a drawn or dragged thing moves with the settlement; to pin a thing in a new place the user drags it there and locks it; (2) locks have TWO independent axes: a PLACEMENT lock holds where a thing is, and its shape still evolves — it takes part in the division and merging of plots and its cosmetics follow the neighbourhood; a separate SHAPE lock holds its shape and plot size (outline and size only: its cosmetics still follow the neighbourhood); (3) where a raised way meets a wall with a shape lock, the shape lock is honoured — the shape-locked wall never changes; the newer thing bends itself, else gives way (by hand refused, at generation not built, at an epoch it waits); a placement-locked wall still reshapes at a meeting (J144). RULE (the owner's, approving the program's proposal): an aqueduct crosses a wall at its own level, in one of three forms — OVER: its channel above the wall's top, it passes on an arch that clears the wall, and may pass over a gate, postern or tower whose top it clears (the gate standing under the arch); MERGED: its channel within the wall's height, the wall is built up to and around the aqueduct's own arch or pier at the crossing and the water passes through that arch (the Porta Maggiore form, not a tunnel bored through the wall); BENEATH: its channel underground, a conduit under the wall. Nothing hard moves (J148). Viaducts and elevated roads are unchanged (they come down before a standing wall and pass through its gate). The owner's condition "if they help" is the program's to apply: a form is written into the laws where it removes more cases than it adds. PROGRAM READING (J122, vetoable): the merged arch is the aqueduct's own masonry, so a wall's decay, breach or dismantling leaves the aqueduct standing (item 114); it is no gate (no way passes it), so no junction (item 110 (f)); a merge never falls on a gate's passage or in a tower — there the aqueduct bends along its course to plain wall (J148, tier 2), else gives way; a shape-locked wall is never merged into (J147) — the aqueduct passes over it or bends, else gives way; a wall laid later than a standing aqueduct takes the same form at the aqueduct's level (built around its arch, or passing beneath an arch that clears it). | the owner's approval; the forms' texts the program's | veto by naming the J row |
